thanks to \eqref{a3.18},
\begin{align}\label{estim Er}
&\int^{T_{0}}_0\|Er^2\|_{L^2}{\rm d} t\lesssim \mathcal{E}_{2,in}\int^{T_{0}}_0(1+t)^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}+\nu+\nu^{\frac23} (1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac{5}{12}}{\rm d} t\lesssim \nu^{\frac13}\ln(1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{2,in}.
\end{align}
Substituting \eqref{a3.53}--\eqref{estim Er} into \eqref{a3.52} yields \eqref{a3.51}, which completes the proof of this proposition.
<!-- end proof-21 -->
<!-- prop-13: body_md -->
\label{ap3.8}
If $0 \leq t \leq T_0 = \nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}$, then
\begin{equation}\label{a3.58}
\|\omega(t)\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}} \|\nabla \omega(t)\|_{L^2L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\theta(t)\|_{X_1} \lesssim\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{in},
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}\label{aa3.58}
\|(1+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t\mathcal{M}_1)^\delta\omega(t)\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|(1+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t\mathcal{M}_1)^\delta\theta(t)\|_{L^\infty L^2} \lesssim\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{in},
\end{equation}
<!-- end prop-13 -->
<!-- proof-22: body_md -->
Firstly, we multiply \eqref{a3.5}$_1$ and \eqref{a3.5}$_2$ by $\omega$ and $\theta$ respectively, and integrate it with respect to $x$ and $y$ to obtain
\[
\frac{1}{2} \frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t} \|\omega\|^2 + \nu \|\nabla \omega\|^2 = \int_{-1}^1\int_\mathbb{R} (- u \cdot \nabla \omega_{a} - Er^1-\partial_x \theta) \omega {\rm d} x {\rm d} y,
\]
\[
\frac{1}{2} \frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t} \|\theta\|^2 + \nu \|\nabla \theta\|^2 = \int_{-1}^1\int_\mathbb{R} (-u \cdot \nabla \theta_{a} - Er^2) \theta {\rm d} x {\rm d} y.
\]
Using $u = (\partial_y, -\partial_x) \phi$ and $\phi = \Delta^{-1} \omega$, we have $\|u\| = \|\nabla \phi\| \leq C \|\omega\|$,
and
\begin{align*}
\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t} \|\omega\|^2+\nu \|\nabla \omega\|^2
&\leq \|\omega\|\|u \cdot \nabla \omega_{a}+Er^1\|+\|\omega\|\| \partial_x\theta\| \\
&\leq \| \omega\|(\|u\|\|\nabla \omega_{a}\|_{L^\infty} + \|Er^1\|) +\|\omega\|\| \partial_x\theta\|\\
&\lesssim\|\omega\|^2\|\nabla \omega_{a}\|_{L^\infty} + \|\omega\|\|Er^1\| + \|\omega\|\| \partial_x\theta\|,
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t} \|\theta\|^2+\nu \|\nabla \theta\|^2
&\leq \|\omega\|\|u \cdot \nabla \theta_{a} +Er^2\| \\
&\leq \| \theta\|(\|u\| \|\nabla \theta_{a}\|_{L^\infty} + \|Er^2\|) \\
&\lesssim\| \theta\|\|\omega\|\|\nabla \theta_{a}\|_{L^\infty} + \|\theta\|\|Er^2\| .
\end{align*}
Integrating the above equations with respect to $t$ yields
\begin{align}
  &\|\omega\|^2_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu \|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2L^2}^2 \notag\\ 
  &\leq C\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}^2\|\nabla \omega_{a}\|_{L^1 L^\infty} + C\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}(\|Er^1\|_{L^1L^2}+\|\partial_x\theta\|_{L^1L^2}),\notag  
\end{align}
\begin{align}
  &\|\theta\|^2_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu \|\nabla \theta\|_{L^2L^2}^2 \notag\\ 
  &\leq C\|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\nabla \theta_{a}\|_{L^1 L^\infty} + C\|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|Er^2\|_{L^1L^2}.\notag  
\end{align}
From \eqref{a3.8}--\eqref{a3.9}, \eqref{omL Linfty}--\eqref{paxomL Linfty} and \eqref{a3.18}--\eqref{ErL2}, we arrive at 
 \begin{align}
  &\|\omega\|^2_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu \|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2L^2}^2 \notag\\ 
  \leq &C\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}^2 + C\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1}) \mathcal{E}_{in}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2} +\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\partial_x\theta\|_{L^1L^2}   \notag\\
  \leq& \left(C\varepsilon_0+\frac{1}{2}\right)\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}^2 +C\nu^{\frac23}\ln^2 (1+\nu^{-1}) \mathcal{E}_{in}^2+C\|\partial_x\theta\|_{L^1L^2}^2,\notag
\end{align}
\begin{align}
 \|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}^2 + \nu \|\nabla \theta\|_{L^2L^2}^2  
 \leq& C \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}\notag\\ 
 \leq& \left(C\varepsilon_0+\frac{1}{2}\right)\|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}^2+C\varepsilon_0\nu\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}^2+C\nu^{\frac23}\ln ^2(1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{2,in}^2.\notag
\end{align}
By the smallness of $\varepsilon_0$, we obtain 
 \begin{align}\notag
  \|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}} \|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2L^2} 
  \leq C\left[\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\theta\|_{X_1}+\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1}) \mathcal{E}_{in}\right],
\end{align}
\begin{align}\notag
 \|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2} + \nu^{\frac{1}{2}} \|\nabla \theta\|_{L^2L^2}  
 \leq C\varepsilon_0^{\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+C\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{2,in}.
\end{align}
Notice that 
\begin{align}\notag
  \nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\theta\|_{X_1}\leq& C\nu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}+C\nu^{-\frac{13}{12}}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\theta\|_{X_1}\notag \\ 
  &+C\nu^{-\frac{9}{8}}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}^{\frac{9}{10}}(\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2 L^2})^{\frac{1}{10}}\|\theta\|_{X_1}+C\nu^{-\frac16}\ln(1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{2,in},\end{align}
then  combining the above three equations shows
\begin{align}\notag
&\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}} \|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}} \|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\theta\|_{X_1}\notag\\ 
\lesssim&\nu^{-\frac{13}{12}}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\theta\|_{X_1}+\nu^{-\frac{9}{8}}\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}^{\frac{9}{10}}(\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2 L^2})^{\frac{1}{10}}\|\theta\|_{X_1}+\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1}) \mathcal{E}_{in}.\notag
\end{align}
Assume that $\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}} \|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\theta\|_{X_1}\leq \varepsilon_1\nu^{\frac{2}{3}}\ln (1+\nu^{-1})$ for some small constant $\varepsilon_1$, then we conclude
\begin{align}\notag
\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}} \|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\theta\|_{X_1}\leq C\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1}) \mathcal{E}_{in}.
\end{align}
Notice that $\mathcal{E}_{in}\leq \varepsilon_0 \nu^{\frac{1}{3}},$
Then for $0 \leq t \leq\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}$,  we have
\[
\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}} \|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\theta\|_{X_1} \leq C\nu^{\frac13}\ln (1+\nu^{-1})\mathcal{E}_{in} \leq C\varepsilon_0\nu^{\frac23}\ln (1+\nu^{-1}).
\]
Assume $\varepsilon_0\leq \frac{\varepsilon_1}{2C}$, we get \eqref{a3.58}. 
Then by $(1+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t|\mathcal{M}_1|)^\delta\lesssim 1$ and the definition of $\|\cdot\|$ in \eqref{x1}, we can also get \eqref{aa3.58}
The proof of this proposition is completed.
<!-- end proof-22 -->
<!-- prop-14: body_md -->
\label{ap3.9}
Assume that $f$ is the solution to \eqref{a3.61} for $t\in [t_0,t_1]$, then, there exists a suitable small constant $\varepsilon$ such that
\begin{align}
\|f\|_{X_2}\lesssim &\|f(t_0)\|_{L^2}+\| g_2\|_{L^1L^2},\label{esti: ftildeX} \\
\nu^{\frac13+\varepsilon}\|\mathcal{M}_1 f\|_{L^1 L^2}\lesssim_{\varepsilon} &\|f(t_0)\|_{L^2}+\|g_2\|_{L^1 L^2}.\label{f enhance}
\end{align}
Moreover, if $t_0\geq \nu^{-1-\varepsilon}$, we have
\begin{align}
\|f\|_{X_{2,\delta}}\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13} t_0 \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}f(t_0)\|_{L^2}+\|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}g_2\|_{L^1 L^2},\label{fX large time}\\
\nu^{\frac13}\|\mathcal{M}_1(1+ \nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}f\|_{L^1L^2}\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13} t_0 \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}f(t_0)\|_{L^2}+\|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}g_2\|_{L^1 L^2}.\label{esti: nu1/3M f}
\end{align}
<!-- end prop-14 -->
<!-- proof-23: body_md -->
See \cite{miao2025} for details.
<!-- end proof-23 -->
<!-- prop-15: body_md -->
\label{ap3.12}
Under the conditions of Theorem \ref{thm1.2}, there exists a constant $0<\varepsilon\ll 1$ such that for $j_0=\log_2 \nu^{-\frac23-\varepsilon}$, 
\begin{align}
\|\omega_*\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\lesssim&  \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac58} \|\omega\|^2_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})} +\nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}, \label{esti om1 tildeX}\\
\|\omega_*\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\lesssim& \|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega_*(T_{j_0})\|_{L^2}+\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)} \notag\\ 
&+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|^2_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)},\label{esti om1 X large}\\
\|\theta_*\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\lesssim&  \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_{0},T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})} \notag\\ 
&+\nu^{\frac56-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}, \label{a3.79}\\
\|\theta_*\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\lesssim &\|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\theta_*(T_{j_0})\|_{L^2}+\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac12}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\notag\\ 
&+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}.\label{a3.80}
\end{align}
<!-- end prop-15 -->
<!-- proof-24: body_md -->
We only give the proof of \eqref{a3.79}--\eqref{a3.80}, and the proof of \eqref{esti om1 tildeX}--\eqref{esti om1 X large} can be given similarly. By using \eqref{esti: ftildeX} with $t_0=T_0$ and Proposition \ref{ap3.8}, we infer 
\begin{align*}
\|\theta_*\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\lesssim \|\theta_*(T_0)\|_{L^2}+\|h_2\|_{L^1(T_0,T_{j_0};L^2)}\lesssim  \nu^{\frac56-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}+\|h_2\|_{L^1(T_0,T_{j_0};L^2)}.
\end{align*}
Then we use \eqref{fX large time} with $t_0=T_{j_0}=2^{j_0}\nu^{-\frac13}=\nu^{-1-\varepsilon}$ to get
\begin{align*}
\|\theta_*\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\lesssim \|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\theta_*(T_{j_0})\|_{L^2}+ \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}h_2\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}.
\end{align*}
In the following, we only need to show
\begin{align}
\|h_2\|_{L^1(T_0,T_{j_0};L^2)}\lesssim & \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_{0},T_{j_0})}\notag\\ 
&+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{\frac56-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in},\label{a3.81}\\
\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}h_2\|_{L^1 (T_{j_0},T; L^2) }\lesssim &\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac12}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\notag\\ 
&+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}.\label{a3.82}
\end{align}

{\it Step 1. The estimate of $u^{(1)}\partial_x\theta_{a}+u^{(2)}(\partial_y+t\partial_x)\theta_{a}$}.
For $t\in (T_{j_0}, T)$, we use Proposition \ref{linear} and $\|u\|\lesssim \|\omega\|$ to obtain
\begin{equation*}
\begin{aligned}
&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u^{(1)}\partial_x\theta_{a}+u^{(2)}(\partial_y+t\partial_x)\theta_{a})\| \\
\lesssim&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u\|(\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\partial_x\theta_{a}\|_{L^\infty} +\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(\partial_y+t\partial_x)\theta_{a}\|_{L^\infty}).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation*}
Integrating in time, we deduce
\begin{align}\label{weight-u1paxomL}
&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u^{(1)}\partial_x\theta_{a}+u^{(2)}(\partial_y+t\partial_x)\theta_{a})\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2) } \notag\\
\lesssim& \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega\|_{L^\infty(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}\|(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac12}\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T)}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\notag\\
\lesssim& \mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}.
\end{align}

For $t\in (T_0, T_{j_0})$, note that
\begin{align*}
\|(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac12}\|_{L^1(T_0,T_{j_0})}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac13}.
\end{align*}
Then we get that
\begin{align}\label{u1epaxom+u2payom}
\|u^{(1)}\partial_x\theta_{a}+u^{(2)}(\partial_y+t\partial_x)\theta_{a}\|_{L^1(T_{0},T_{j_0}; L^2)}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_{0},T_{j_0})}.
\end{align}

{\it Step 2. The estimate of $U\cdot\nabla \theta$}.
Notice that
$$U\cdot\nabla \theta=u\cdot\nabla \theta+u_{a}\cdot\nabla \theta.$$
For $u_{a}\cdot\nabla \theta$, we infer
\begin{align}\label{uLomeL1L2}
&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u_{a}\cdot\nabla \theta)\|_{ L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}\notag\\
\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u_{a}^{(1)}\partial_x \theta)\|_{ L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u_{a}^{(2)}\partial_y \theta)\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}\notag\\
\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u_{a}^{(1)}\|_{L^\infty(T_{j_0},T; L^\infty)}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\partial_x \theta\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}\notag\\
&+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u_{a}^{(2)}\|_{L^2(T_{j_0},T;L^\infty)}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\partial_y \theta\|_{L^2(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}.
\end{align}
Using \eqref{u1LLinfty}--\eqref{u12L22}, we obtain
\begin{equation*}
\begin{aligned}
&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u_{a}^{(1)}\|_{L^\infty(T_{j_0},T; L^\infty)}\lesssim T^{-1}_{j_0}\mathcal{E}_{in}\lesssim \nu \mathcal{E}_{in},\\
&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u_{a}^{(2)}\|_{L^2(T_{j_0},T;L^\infty)}\lesssim \|(1+t)^{-2}\ln(1+t)\|_{L^2(T_{j_0}, T)} \mathcal{E}_{in}\lesssim \nu^{\frac32}\mathcal{E}_{in},
\end{aligned}
\end{equation*}
which together with \eqref{uLomeL1L2} gives
\begin{align}\label{weight-uLnaome}
\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u_{a}\cdot\nabla \theta)\|_{ L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}
\lesssim \nu^{\frac12}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}.
\end{align}
When $t\in (T_0,T_{j_0})$, 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{aligned}
&\|u_{a}^{(1)}\|_{L^\infty(T_0,T_{j_0}; L^\infty)}\lesssim T^{-1}_{0}\mathcal{E}_{in}\lesssim \nu^{\frac16} \mathcal{E}_{in},\\
&\|u_{a}^{(2)}\|_{L^2(T_0,T_{j_0};L^\infty)}\lesssim \|(1+t)^{-2}\ln(1+t)\|_{L^2(T_{0}, T_{j_0})} \mathcal{E}_{in}\lesssim \nu^{\frac16}\mathcal{E}_{in},
\end{aligned}
\end{equation*}
and then,
\begin{align}\label{uLnaome}
\|u_{a}\cdot\nabla \theta\|_{ L^1(T_{0},T_{j_0}; L^2)}
\lesssim \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_{0},T_{j_0})}.
\end{align}
For the estimate of  $u\cdot\nabla \theta$, we get
\begin{align*}
&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u\cdot\nabla \theta)\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}\\
\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u^{(1)} \partial_x\theta)\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u^{(2)}\partial_y \theta)\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T; L^2)}\\
\lesssim &\big\|\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u^{(1)}\|_{L^\infty} \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\partial_x\theta\|_{L^2}\big\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T)}\\
&+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u^{(2)}\|_{L^2(T_{j_0},T; L^\infty)}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\partial_y \theta\|_{L^2(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}
:=I_1+I_2.
\end{align*}
Notice that
\begin{align*}
\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u^{(1)}\|_{L^\infty}\lesssim \int_{\mathbb{R}}(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k) )^{\delta}\|u^{(1)}_{k}\|_{L^\infty_y} {\rm d} k.
\end{align*}
If $|k|\leq 1$, we apply $\|u^{(1)}_{k}\|_{L^\infty_y}\lesssim \|\omega_{k}\|_{L^2_y}$ to obtain 
\begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
\int_{|k|\leq1}(1+\nu^{\frac13} t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\|u^{(1)}_{k}\|_{L^\infty_y} {\rm d} k\lesssim \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega\|.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
If $|k|\geq 1$, as in \cite{miao2025}, we have 
\begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
\int_{|k|\geq1}(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\|u^{(1)}_{k}\|_{L^\infty_y}{\rm d} k
\lesssim  \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}k u^{(1)}_{k}\|^{\frac34}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\partial_y \omega_{k}\|^{\frac14}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
By combining the above three formulas, we can get
\begin{align}\label{esti I}
I_1\lesssim& \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega\|_{L^\infty(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\partial_x\theta\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}\notag\\
&+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\mathcal{M}_2\nabla \Delta^{-1}\omega\|^{\frac34}_{L^2(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\nabla  \omega\|^{\frac14}_{L^2(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}\notag\\ 
&\ \ \ \ \times\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\nabla  \theta\|_{L^2(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}\notag\\
\lesssim &\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}.
\end{align}
For $I_2$, note that $\phi_k=(\partial^2_y-k^2)^{-1}\omega_k$ and
\begin{align*}
\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}u^{(2)}\|_{L^\infty}\lesssim \int_{\mathbb{R}}(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\|u^{(2)}_{k}\|_{L^\infty_y} {\rm d} k.
\end{align*}
If $|k|\leq 1$,  it holds
\begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
&\int_{|k|\leq1}(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\|u^{(2)}_{k}\|_{L^\infty_y}{\rm d} k\\
\lesssim &\int_{|k|\leq 1} |k|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\|u_k\|^{\frac34}_{L^2_y}\|\partial_y^2u_k\|^{\frac14}_{L^2_y} {\rm d} k\\
\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta} |k|^{\frac12} u_k\|^{\frac34}_{L^2_kL^2_y}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\partial_y \omega_{k}\|^{\frac14}_{L^2_kL^2_y}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
If $|k|\geq 1$, we can also prove that
\begin{equation}
\begin{aligned}\notag
&\int_{|k|\geq1}(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\|u^{(2)}_{k}\|_{L^\infty_y}dk\\
\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}k u^{(2)}_{k}\|^{\frac34}_{L^2_kL^2_y}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1(k))^{\delta}\partial_y \omega_{k}\|^{\frac14}_{L^2_kL^2_y},
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
then we arrive at
\begin{align}\label{esti II}
I_2\lesssim \nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}.
\end{align}
Combining  \eqref{esti I}--\eqref{esti II}, we have
\begin{align}\label{weight-uenaome}
\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}(u\cdot\nabla \theta)\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}\lesssim\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)} .
\end{align}
When $t\in (T_0,T_{j_0})$, similar to \eqref{esti I}--\eqref{esti II}, we derive
\begin{align}\label{uenaome}
\|u\cdot\nabla \theta\|_{L^1(T_0,T_{j_0};L^2)}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}.
\end{align}
{\it Step 3. The estimate of $E_r^2$.}
We use $(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac14}\lesssim (\nu^{\frac13}(1+t))^{-\varepsilon}$ and $(\nu(1+t))^{\frac13}{\rm e}^{-\nu t}\lesssim 1$ to get
\begin{align*}
&\|{\rm e}^{-\nu t}(1+t)^{-1}(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac14}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T)}+\|\nu {\rm e}^{-\nu t}(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac14}\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T)}\\
\lesssim  & \|\nu^{\frac13}(1+t)^{-1}(\nu^{\frac13}(1+t))^{-\varepsilon}+\nu^{\frac23}(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac14}(1+t)^{-\frac13}\|_{L^1(T_{j_0},T)}
\lesssim \nu^{\frac13},
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\|{\rm e}^{-\nu t}(1+t)^{-1}(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac14}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|_{L^1(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\|\nu {\rm e}^{-\nu t}(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac14}\|_{L^1(T_0, T_{j_0})}\lesssim \nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon},
\end{align*}
and
\begin{align*}
&\int_{T_{0}}^{T} \nu^{\frac23} {\rm e}^{-\nu t}(1+\nu t^3)^{-\frac{5}{12}}{\rm d} t\lesssim \nu^{\frac13}.
\end{align*}
Then by \eqref{a3.18}, we obtain
\begin{align}
&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}E_r^2\|_{ L^1(T_{j_0},T;L^2)}\lesssim \nu^{\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in},\label{weight-Er}\\
&\|E_r^2\|_{ L^1(T_0,T_{j_0};L^2)}\lesssim \nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\lesssim\nu^{\frac56-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}.\label{estim Er}
\end{align}
By combining \eqref{weight-u1paxomL}--\eqref{estim Er}, we get \eqref{a3.81}--\eqref{a3.82}, and then finish the proof of Proposition \ref{ap3.12}.
<!-- end proof-24 -->
<!-- prop-16: body_md -->
\label{pro: wre}
Under the conditions of Theorem \ref{thm1.2}, it holds that for $j_0=\log_2 \nu^{-\frac23-\varepsilon}$, 
\begin{align}
\|\omega_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\lesssim &\nu^{-\frac13}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\mathcal{E}_{in},\label{main result}\\
\|\omega_{re}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\lesssim &\nu^{-\frac13}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\mathcal{E}_{in}+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta} \omega_{re}(T_{j_0})\|,\label{om2 Xtj}\\ 
\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\lesssim &\nu^{-\frac13}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\mathcal{E}_{2,in},\label{a3.96}\\
\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\lesssim &\nu^{-\frac13}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta} \theta_{re}(T_{j_0})\|.\label{a3.97}
\end{align}
<!-- end prop-16 -->
<!-- lem-13: body_md -->
\label{al3.14}
Under the conditions of Theorem \ref{thm1.2}, the solution $\theta_{re,j}$ to \eqref{equ: om2j} satisfies
\begin{align}
\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j-1},T_j)}\lesssim2^{-\frac{j}{4}}\nu^{-\frac13}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\mathcal{M}_2\nabla\Delta^{-1}\omega\|_{L^2(T_{j-1},T_j;L^2)} \mathcal{E}_{2,in}, \quad j\geq 1. \label{om2j large}
\end{align}
In particular, for $\delta=0$, it holds that
\begin{align}
\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_2(T_{j-1},T_j)}\lesssim2^{-\frac{j}{4}}\nu^{-\frac13}\|\mathcal{M}_2\nabla\Delta^{-1}\omega\|_{L^2(T_{j-1},T_j;L^2)} \mathcal{E}_{2,in}, \quad j\geq 1. \label{om2j small}
\end{align}
<!-- end lem-13 -->
<!-- proof-25: body_md -->
See Proposition 3.7 in \cite{miao2025}.
<!-- end proof-25 -->
<!-- proof-26: body_md -->
For \eqref{a3.96}, we use $\theta_{re}=\sum_{j}\theta_{re,j}$ to get 
\begin{align}\label{om2XT0Tj0}
\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}=&\Big\|\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_0} \theta_{re,j}\Big\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\leq \sum_{1\leq j\leq j_0 }\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_2(T_{j-1},T_{j_0})}\notag\\
= &\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_0 }\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_2(T_{j-1},T_j)}+\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_0-1}\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_2(T_{j},T_{j_0})}.
\end{align}
Then by \eqref{om2j small}, we infer
\begin{align}\label{om2jtildeXj}
\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_0 }\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_2(T_{j-1},T_j)}&\lesssim \nu^{-\frac13}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_0}2^{-\frac{j}{4}}\|\mathcal{M}_2 \nabla\Delta^{-1}\omega\|_{L^2(T_{j-1},T_j;L^2)} \mathcal{E}_{2,in}\notag\\ 
&\lesssim \nu^{-\frac13}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}.
\end{align}
For $t>T_j$, the system \eqref{equ: om2j} becomes
\begin{equation}\label{equ: om2j free}
\left\{
\begin{aligned}
&\partial_t \theta_{re,j}+y\partial_x\theta_{re,j}-\nu\Delta\theta_{re,j}=0, \quad t>T_{j}, \\
& \theta_{re,j}(t,x,\pm1)=0, \quad \theta_{re,j}|_{t=T_{j}}=\theta_{re,j}(T_j).
\end{aligned}
\right.
\end{equation}
By \eqref{esti: ftildeX} and \eqref{om2j small}, we obtain
\begin{align}\label{om2j Tj-Tj0}
\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_2(T_{j},T_{j_0})}\lesssim&\|\theta_{re,j}(T_j)\|_{L^2}\lesssim\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_2(T_{j-1},T_j)}\notag\\ 
\lesssim&2^{-\frac{j}{4}}\nu^{-\frac13}\|\mathcal{M}_2 \nabla\Delta^{-1}\omega\|_{L^2(T_{j-1},T_j;L^2)} \mathcal{E}_{2,in}.
\end{align}
Putting \eqref{om2jtildeXj} and \eqref{om2j Tj-Tj0} into \eqref{om2XT0Tj0}, we have
\begin{align}
\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\lesssim\nu^{-\frac13}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}.
\end{align}

For \eqref{a3.97}, define
$$\theta_{re}=\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_0}\theta_{re,j}+\sum_{j\geq j_0+1}\theta_{re,j}:=\widetilde{\theta}_{re}+\sum_{j\geq j_0+1}\theta_{re,j}.$$
Here $\widetilde{\theta}_{re}$ satisfies
\begin{equation*}
\left\{
\begin{aligned}
&\partial_t \widetilde{\theta}_{re}-\nu\Delta\widetilde{\theta}_{re}+y\partial_x\widetilde{\theta}_{re}=0, \quad t>T_{j_0}, \\
& \widetilde{\theta}_{re}(t,x,\pm1)=0, \quad \widetilde{\theta}_{re}|_{t=T_{j_0}}=\theta_{re}(T_{j_0}).
\end{aligned}
\right.
\end{equation*}
It follows from \eqref{fX large time} that
\begin{align}\label{om2,j small}
\|\widetilde{\theta}_{re}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\lesssim &\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta} \widetilde{\theta}_{re}(T_{j_0})\|\lesssim\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta} \theta_{re}(T_{j_0})\|.
\end{align}
For $j\geq j_0+1$, similar to \eqref{om2jtildeXj}--\eqref{om2j Tj-Tj0}, we apply \eqref{om2j large} to obtain
\begin{align*}
\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j-1},T)}\leq &\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j-1},T_j)}+\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_j,T)}\\
\lesssim &2^{-\frac{j}{4}}\nu^{-\frac13}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\mathcal{M}_2 \nabla\Delta^{-1}\omega\|_{L^2(T_{j-1},T_j;L^2)}\mathcal{E}_{2,in},
\end{align*}
which implies
\begin{align}\label{om2,j large}
\sum_{j\geq j_0+1}\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j},T)}\lesssim\nu^{-\frac13}\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\mathcal{M}_2 \nabla\Delta^{-1}\omega\|_{L^2(T_{j_0},T;L^2)} \mathcal{E}_{2,in}.
\end{align}
Combining \eqref{om2,j small}--\eqref{om2,j large}, we infer
\begin{align*}
\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\leq &\|\widetilde{\theta}_{re}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\sum_{j\geq j_0+1}\|\theta_{re,j}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j-1},T)}\\
\lesssim &\nu^{-\frac13}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0}, T)}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta} \theta_{re}(T_{j_0})\|.
\end{align*}
Therefore, we have \eqref{a3.96}--\eqref{a3.97}, and the proof of \eqref{main result}--\eqref{om2 Xtj} is similar. The proof of Proposition \ref{pro: wre} is completed.
<!-- end proof-26 -->
<!-- proof-27: body_md -->
Using \eqref{esti om1 tildeX}--\eqref{a3.80} and \eqref{main result}--\eqref{a3.97}, we infer
\begin{align*}
\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\leq& \|\omega_*\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\|\omega_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\\
\lesssim& \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|^2_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})},\\ 
\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\leq& \|\theta_*\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\\
\lesssim& \nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_{0},T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})} \notag\\ 
& +\nu^{\frac56-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}.
\end{align*}
Notice that $\nu^{-\frac12}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}+\mathcal{E}_{in}\leq \varepsilon_0\nu^{\frac13}$ and $\varepsilon_0\ll 1$, 
\begin{align*}
\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\leq& C(\nu^{-\frac58} \|\omega\|^2_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}),\\ 
\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\leq&C(\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{\frac56-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}),
\end{align*}
which implies that
\begin{align*}
\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\lesssim& \nu^{-\frac58} \|\omega\|^2_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac98}\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0,T_{j_0})}+\nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}.
\end{align*}
We use $\mathcal{E}_{in}\leq \varepsilon_0\nu^{\frac13}$ and a priori assumption $\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\leq \varepsilon_1\nu^{\frac23-\varepsilon}$ to get
\begin{align*}
\|\omega\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\lesssim\nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}.
\end{align*}
Putting it into \eqref{esti om1 tildeX}, \eqref{a3.79}, \eqref{main result} and \eqref{a3.96} gives
\begin{align*}
\|\omega_*\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\|\omega_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\nu^{-\frac12}(\|\theta_*\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})})\lesssim\nu^{\frac13-\varepsilon}\mathcal{E}_{in}.
\end{align*}
By taking $0< \varepsilon<(\frac{1}{24}-\frac23\delta)(1+\delta)^{-1}$ and using $T_{j_0}=\nu^{-1-\varepsilon}$, we have
\begin{align}\label{ome small}
&\|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\theta_*(T_{j_0})\|\leq  \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\theta_* \|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\notag\\
\leq & \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t)^{\delta}\|_{L^\infty(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta_*\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}
\leq  C \nu^{\frac13-\frac23\delta-\varepsilon(1+\delta)}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\lesssim\nu^{\frac{19}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in},
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{om2 small}
&\|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\theta_{re}(T_{j_0})\|\leq  \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\theta_{re} \|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\notag\\
\leq & \|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t)^{\delta}\|_{L^\infty(T_0,T_{j_0})}\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}
\leq  C \nu^{\frac13-\frac23\delta-\varepsilon(1+\delta)}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\lesssim\nu^{\frac{19}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in},
\end{align}
and
\begin{align}\label{a3.125}
  &\|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega_*(T_{j_0})\|+\|(1+ \nu^{\frac13}T_{j_0} \mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega_{re}(T_{j_0})\|\notag\\ 
  \lesssim&\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega_* \|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}+\|(1+\nu^{\frac13}t\mathcal{M}_1)^{\delta}\omega_{re} \|_{X_2(T_0, T_{j_0})}\lesssim\nu^{\frac{7}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in}.
\end{align}
Then by \eqref{1.8}, \eqref{esti om1 X large}, \eqref{a3.80}, \eqref{om2 Xtj}, \eqref{a3.97}, \eqref{ome small}--\eqref{a3.125}, we conclude
\begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\leq& \|\omega_*\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\|\omega_{re}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\\
\leq &C(\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|^2_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac{7}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in})\\
\leq &C(\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|^2_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac{7}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in}),\\
\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\leq& \|\theta_*\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\|\theta_{re}\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\\
\leq &C(\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{in}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac13}\mathcal{E}_{2,in}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\\ 
&+\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{\frac{19}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in}).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
Therefore,
\begin{align}
 \|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\lesssim&\nu^{-\frac58}\|\omega\|^2_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac98}\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\notag\\ 
 &+\nu^{\frac{7}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in}.   
\end{align}
By assuming $\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)} \leq \varepsilon_1\nu^{\frac58}$, and using $\mathcal{E}_{in}\leq \varepsilon_0\nu^{\frac13}~(\varepsilon_0\ll 1)$, we arrive at
\begin{align}\label{ome X}
\|\omega\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}+\nu^{-\frac12}\|\theta\|_{X_{2,\delta}(T_{j_0},T)}\leq C \nu^{\frac{7}{24}}\mathcal{E}_{in},
\end{align}
which together with Proposition \ref{ap3.8} and \eqref{ome small}--\eqref{a3.125} gives \eqref{a3.20}--\eqref{a3.20a}. This completes the proof of Proposition \ref{ap3.2}.
<!-- end proof-27 -->
<!-- thm-3: body_md -->
\label{thm4.1}
    There exist $0<\delta, \varepsilon_0\ll 1$ such that for all $0<\nu \le 1$, $(x,y)\in {\mathbb T}\times[-1,1]$, if the initial data $\Omega_{in}, \Theta_{in}$ satisfies $\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}\le \varepsilon_0\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}$, $(\omega^i,\theta^i)(t,x,\pm1)=0$, then the solution $(\omega^i,\theta^i)(t,x,y)$ of \eqref{i} satisfies the following stability estimates:
    \[\|\omega^i_{\neq}\|+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\theta^i_{\neq}\|\lesssim {\rm e}^{-\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}),\]
    \[\|u^i_{\neq}\|\lesssim (1+t)^{-1} {\rm e}^{-\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t/2}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}).\]
<!-- end thm-3 -->
<!-- lem-14: body_md -->
\label{lem:A.1}
Suppose that $k\in \mathbb{Z}$ and $P_0 f = 0$, then for a constant $0<\delta_1< 1$, it holds that
\[
\|f\|_{L^\infty} \lesssim \min\left\{\frac{1}{\delta_1}\|\nabla f\|^{1-\frac{\delta_1}{2}} \|\partial_x^2f\|^{\frac{\delta_1}{2}},\ \|\partial_x f\|^{\frac{3}{4}} \|f\|_{H^2}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right\}.
\]
<!-- end lem-14 -->
<!-- proof-28: body_md -->
Denote $f_k(y) = \frac{1}{2\pi} \int_{\mathbb{T}} f(x,y) {\rm e}^{-ikx} {\rm d} x, I=[-1,1]$, then for $k \in \mathbb{Z}$ and $j=1,2$,
\[
f(x,y) = \sum_{k \neq 0} f_k(y) {\rm e}^{ikx},
\]
and
\[
\|\partial_x^j f\|_{L^2(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R})}^2 = 2\pi \sum_{k \neq 0} \|(ik)^j f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^2, \quad
\|\nabla f\|_{L^2(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R})}^2 = 2\pi \sum_{k \neq 0} \|(k, \partial_y) f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^2.
\]
Using Sobolev inequality and Hölder inequality gives
\begin{align*}
\|f\|_{L^\infty(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R})} &\leq \sum_{k \neq 0} \|f_k\|_{L^\infty(I)} \lesssim \sum_{k \neq 0} \|f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^{\frac{1}{2}} \|f_k\|_{H^1(I)}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim \sum_{k \neq 0} |k|^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\delta_1}{2}}\||k|^{1+\delta_1}f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^{\frac{1}{2}} \|f_k\|_{H^1(I)}^{\frac{1}{2}} \\
&\lesssim \left( \sum_{k \neq 0} |k|^{-1-\delta_1} \right)^{\frac{1}{2}} \left( \sum_{k \neq 0} \||k|^{1+\delta_1} f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^2 \right)^{\frac{1}{4}} \left( \sum_{k \neq 0} \|(k, \partial_y) f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^2 \right)^{\frac{1}{4}} \\
&\lesssim \frac{1}{\delta_1} \left( \sum_{k \neq 0} \||k|^{2} f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^{2\delta_1}\||k|f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^{2(1-\delta_1)} \right)^{\frac{1}{4}} \left( \sum_{k \neq 0} \|(k, \partial_y) f_k\|_{L^2(I)}^2 \right)^{\frac{1}{4}} \\
&\lesssim \frac{1}{\delta_1} \|\nabla f\|^{1-\frac{\delta_1}{2}} \|\partial_x^2f\|^{\frac{\delta_1}{2}}.
\end{align*}
The proof of $\|f\|_{L^\infty} \lesssim \|\partial_x f\|^{\frac{3}{4}} \|f\|_{H^2}^{\frac{1}{4}}$ was shown in \cite{wei-zhang2026}. The proof of this lemma is completed.
<!-- end proof-28 -->
<!-- prop-17: body_md -->
\label{prop:linear}
 the solution $(\omega_a,\theta_a)$ of \eqref{linear-om}  satisfies that for $t \geq 0$,
\begin{align}
& \|\partial^2_x \theta_a(t)\|_{L^\infty} + \|(\partial_y + t\partial_x)\partial_x\theta_a(t)\|_{L^\infty} \lesssim {\rm e}^{-2\nu^{\frac{1}{3}} t} \|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}, \\
&\|\partial_x Er^2(t)\|\lesssim\ \nu (1+t) {\rm e}^{-2\nu^{\frac{1}{3}} t} \|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}. 
\end{align}
<!-- end prop-17 -->
<!-- proof-29: body_md -->
The proof is similar to that of Proposition \ref{p2.1} and Lemma \ref{l3.2}, and the specific details are omitted here.
<!-- end proof-29 -->
<!-- prop-18: body_md -->
\label{thm:error-term}
    Under the conditions of Theorem \ref{thm4.1}, it holds for $t\ge 0$,
    \[\|\omega_{\neq}\|+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}(\|\theta_{\neq}\|+\|\partial_x\theta_{\neq}\|)\lesssim {\rm e}^{-\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}).\]
<!-- end prop-18 -->
<!-- lem-15: body_md -->
\label{prop:short-time}
    If $0\le t\le \nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}$, then
    \[\|\omega\|+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\theta\|+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_x\theta\|\lesssim\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}).\]
<!-- end lem-15 -->
<!-- proof-30: body_md -->
By energy estimate, we have 
    \begin{equation}\notag
\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2 L^2}\lesssim (\|V_1''\|_{L^1L^\infty}+\|\nabla \omega_a\|_{L^1 L^\infty})+\|Er^1\|_{L^1L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_x \theta\|_{L^\infty L^2},
    \end{equation}
    \begin{equation}\notag
\|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^2L^2}\lesssim\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\partial_x\theta_a\|_{L^1L^\infty}+\|Er^2\|_{L^1L^2} ,
    \end{equation}
    \begin{equation}\notag
\|\partial_x\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\partial_x\theta\|_{L^2L^2}\lesssim \|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|\partial^2_x\theta_a\|_{L^1L^\infty}+\|\partial_x Er^2\|_{L^1L^2} .
    \end{equation}
By Propositions \ref{p2.1} and \ref{prop:linear}, and $0\le t\le \nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}$, we have
    \[\|V_1''\|_{L^1 L^\infty}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4},\ \|\partial_x\theta_a\|_{L^1 L^\infty}+\|\partial_x^2\theta_a\|_{L^1 L^\infty}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4},\]
    \[\|\nabla\omega_a\|_{L^1 L^\infty}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta\|_{H^4}),\  \|Er^1\|_{L^1L^2}\lesssim \nu^{\frac{1}{3}}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta\|_{H^4}),\]
    \[\|Er^2\|_{L^1 L^2}+\|\partial_x Er^2\|_{L^1 L^2}\lesssim \nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}.\]
    Then by assuming that
    \begin{equation}\notag
\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla \omega\|_{L^2 L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\Big( \|\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^2L^2} +\|\partial_x\theta\|_{L^\infty L^2}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\partial_x\theta\|_{L^2L^2}\Big)\lesssim \varepsilon_1 \nu^{\frac{2}{3}}
    \end{equation}
    for some small $\varepsilon_1$ , we have for $0<t\le \nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}$ that
    \[\|\omega\|_{L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\theta\|_{L^2}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_x\theta\|_{L^2}\le C\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}).\]
<!-- end proof-30 -->
<!-- lem-16: body_md -->
\label{prop:reaction}
    It holds that
    \[\|\omega_{re}\|_{X_2}\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla u^{(2)}\|_{L^2 L^2}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}).\]
   Here the norm $\|\cdot \|_{X_2}$ is defined in \eqref{4.15aaa}.
<!-- end lem-16 -->
<!-- proof-31: body_md -->
The proof is similar to Proposition \ref{p4.12a}, and the specific details are omitted here.
<!-- end proof-31 -->
<!-- lem-17: body_md -->
\label{prop:source-estimate}
     It holds that 
     \begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
\|e^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}g\|_{L^1 L^2}\lesssim&\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}(\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4})\|\omega\|_{X_2}+\nu^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|\omega\|_{X_2}^2+\nu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\|\omega\|_{X_2}\|\partial_x\theta\|_{X_2}\\ 
&+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\Omega_{in}\|_{H^4}+\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
<!-- end lem-17 -->
<!-- proof-32: body_md -->
We only estimate $\|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_x\theta\|_{L^1L^2}$; the estimates of other terms are similar to Proposition \ref{prop:2.6}.
    By Proposition \ref{p4.8a}, we have
\[\|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_x\theta\|_{L^1L^2}\le \nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_x\theta\|_{X_2}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\Big(\|\partial_x\theta(T_0)\|_{L^2}+\|e^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}g_1\|_{L^1 L^2}\Big),\]
in which
\[g_1=u_{0}^{(1)}\partial^2_x\theta+u_{0}^{(1)}\partial_x^2\theta_a+\partial_x Er^2.\]
We can prove
\begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
    \|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{0}^{(1)}\partial_x^2\theta\|_{L^1 L^2}&\le\int_{T_0}^T \|u^{(1)}_{0}\|_{L^\infty}\|\partial_x^2\theta\|{\rm d} t\lesssim \int_{T_0}^T \|u_{0}^{(1)}\|_{H^1}\|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial^2_x\theta\|_{L^2}{\rm d} t\\ &\lesssim \int_{T_0}^T \|u_{0}^{(1)}\|_{H^1}\|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial^2_x\theta\|_{L^2}{\rm d} t\lesssim\|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\|e^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_x^2\theta\|_{L^1 L^2}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\omega\|_{X_2}\|\partial_x\theta\|_{X_2}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
By Proposition \ref{p2.1} and Lemma \ref{l3.2}, we get 
\begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
    \|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{0}^{(1)}\partial_x^2\theta_a\|_{L^1 L^2}&\le \int_{T_0}^T{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\|u_{0}^{(1)}\|\|\partial^2_x\theta_a\|_{L^\infty}{\rm d} t\lesssim \|\omega\|_{L^\infty L^2}\int_{T_0}^T {\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}{\rm e}^{-\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4} {\rm d} t\\&\lesssim \nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\omega\|_{X_2}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4},
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
and
\begin{equation}\notag
\begin{aligned}
    \|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_x Er^2\|_{L^1 L^2}=\int_{T_0}^T {\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\|\partial_x Er^2\|{\rm d} t\lesssim \nu\int_{T_0}^T(1+t){\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}{\rm e}^{-2\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}{\rm d} t\le \nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
Above all,
\[\|{\rm e}^{\delta\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_x\theta\|_{L^1 L^2}\lesssim \nu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\|\omega\|_{X_2}\|\partial_x\theta\|_{X_2}+\nu^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|\omega\|_{X_2}\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}+\|\Theta_{in}\|_{H^4}.\]
<!-- end proof-32 -->
<!-- proof-33: body_md -->
The proof is similar to that of Proposition \ref{p2.2}, and the specific details are omitted here.
<!-- end proof-33 -->
<!-- proof-34: body_md -->
Combining Propositions \ref{prop:linear} and \ref{thm:error-term}, we can get the proof of Theorem \ref{thm4.1} immediately.
<!-- end proof-34 -->
